Teams, roles and users each get a device limit edited with the workspace device picker; device lists show only what a user is allowed. The device limit filters the app, it does not block device control through the API. Managers now administer the workspaces of their own teams. Additive team grants are gone: existing team permission lists are reset to *no limit* · `c863dcab4` · 🗄 DB migration `20260929d_access_ceilings.sql`
- **Grafana dashboards show each viewer only their own devices** — every dashboard matches the signed-in Grafana user to their VirtualPyTest account by email and applies their team, role and user device limits in the background; a new **Workspace** drop-down narrows further, and links from the app open on the active workspace. Admins and Grafana's built-in admin see everything; an account with no VirtualPyTest profile sees nothing. Everyone except admins is now a Grafana **Viewer**, so only admins can edit a panel's query. A filter on what dashboards show, not an access boundary · `68f63335b` · 🗄 DB migration `20260929f_grafana_device_scope.sql`
- **Docker host desktop is XFCE** — the `virtualpytest-host` image now starts the same XFCE desktop the native installer sets up, instead of a bare fluxbox, so its VNC tile matches a native host
- **Login page can be reduced to email + password** — for sites where admins create every account: `VITE_ENABLE_SIGNUP=false` hides the Sign Up tab and "Create Account" link, `VITE_ENABLE_RESET_PASSWORD=false` the Reset Password tab, and the existing `VITE_ENABLE_OAUTH=false` (`d9825abf5`) the Google / GitHub buttons. All default to `true`, so nothing changes unless set. Hiding the tab does not close the Supabase signup API — also set `[auth] enable_signup = false` on the Supabase side (not `[auth.email]`, which disables email sign-in) ([setup §16](../get-started/supabase-auth-setup.md#16-login-page-options-admin-provisioned-sites)) · `547933bc8`
- **Test Prompt: open the linked testcase in QuickTest or the Test Builder** — in *Run graph* mode the linked-testcase chip has **QuickTest** and **Test Builder** buttons that open it in a new tab, so the page keeps its Take Control. Both builders now open a testcase from a link (`?testcase=<id>`), which also makes the agent chat's "open test case" link load it. QuickTest opens step lists only and says so for a branching graph (TASK-36)
- **Change a test by prompt in QuickTest and the Test Builder** — describe the change ("add a check that the cart icon is shown"), review the proposed steps (new ones marked, removed ones listed) and Apply. QuickTest has a prompt bar above its steps; the Test Builder's AI tab has *New graph* / *Edit open graph*. Works on step-list tests (every QuickTest test and every AI-generated graph); a branching graph says why it cannot be edited. Steps the change does not touch are kept exactly, references and thresholds included. Uses the `text.plan` AI route (TASK-36)
- **Test Prompt: save a passing exploration as a testcase** — after an Explore run passes, its card offers *Save as testcase*: the steps the agent took (navigations and device actions, without the screen reads) become a step list, the AI adds checks for the acceptance criteria, and you review the proposal before it is saved and linked to the prompt. *Run graph* then replays it without the agent. Needs migration `20261001h` (TASK-36)
- **Test Prompt: each run shows what it ran and how it ended** — expand a result to see the target, prompt and acceptance criteria that actually ran (the form's values, saved or not; the version chip says *edited* when they differed from the saved version), the device, the graph a graph run replayed or generated, the agent's verdict with its evidence, and the error of a failed or refused run. Before, every past run showed the prompt's current version, the verdict and errors were gone after a reload, and an Error card could not be opened. Needs migration `20261001i` (TASK-36)
- **Test Prompt: Auto mode** — *Auto* replays the prompt's saved testcase; if it fails, the agent explores instead, as a second run shown right under the first. A decision model (Jev) also judges why the saved test failed (the app, an out-of-date test, or a glitch) and its pick is shown on the card; for now it is only logged, so its accuracy can be measured against what the exploration finds before it is allowed to decide (TASK-36)
- **Test Prompt: every run starts at home, and stops there if home can't be reached** — Explore goes to the interface's `home` first, then to the target screen; a graph run (generated or replayed) gets a go-home step first. Reaching home is what wakes the device and launches the app, so when it fails (box off, app not launched, screensaver) the prompt does not run: the run ends as an error saying so, and Auto does not explore it (TASK-36)
- **Test Prompt understands the interface's actions** — Explore and Run graph are now told each action node of the tree (e.g. `live_chup` on `live`) with its description, and that visiting it performs the action once, so a prompt like "zap 3 times" maps to three channel-ups. The channel up/down nodes of the TV interfaces got their descriptions ("Zap: change to the next / previous channel") (TASK-36)

### Bug fixes
- **Group tile UX batch 2 (TASK-76 / BUG-0440)** — short tooltips on the `Group control` and `Edit` header buttons ("Take control of several devices together" / "Edit tag"), the page-level View menu no longer offers `Focus + right` / `Focus + below` (the focus layouts still drive the group tile's auto-focus), the per-stream chip is now `Selected` to match the rest of the app, the focused cell's bottom info row is one line (the error/ack chip is hidden on the focused cell so it can't push to two lines), the modal's right pane drops the `Sends to N devices` line and the `press_key → n/m · ms` debug log, the `GroupSelectBar` hint gets a fixed 40 px height (scrolls internally so the bar never reflows on a key press), and `Unfocus` now restores the layout that was active before the focus · [BUG-0440](../bugs/BUG-0440-2026-10-09-group-tile-ux-batch-2.md)
- **Device Control: Group control sits to the left of Edit, and both buttons stay at the same x/y when toggled** — TASK-76's "Group control" rendered after "Edit" and was hidden whenever edit or group mode was active, so the row reflowed on every click. The two buttons now share one row at `height: 40` + `alignSelf: flex-start`, always rendered, in the order `[Group control] [Edit]`; the Edit label flips to "Cancel" in place. Also codified as a UI/UX rule in `UI_STYLE.md` §5 and §9.6 so the next page header doesn't repeat it · [BUG-0438](../bugs/BUG-0438-2026-10-09-device-control-group-control-align-and-stay.md)
- **Device Control: Edit and Cancel sit at the same x/y; grid no longer flings the page** — toggling Edit → Cancel no longer snaps the button one row up: Edit now uses `height: 40` + `alignSelf: flex-start` so it lands on the same line Cancel already occupied. The device-grid scroll box gets `overscroll-behavior: contain` on mobile, so a flick stays in the grid instead of rubber-banding the whole page · [BUG-0437](../bugs/BUG-0437-2026-10-08-device-control-edit-cancel-jump-and-fling.md)
- **vpt-vnc no longer crash-loops on a host whose `/var/lib/vpt_user` is not owned by vpt_user** — TigerVNC was restart-looping every ~30 s on the Minisforum and any other host whose service-user home was created outside `useradd -m` (template clone, snapshot, manual `mkdir`): xauth timed out locking `.Xauthority`, xfce4 couldn't create `~/.cache`, xfconfd lost its dbus name, Xtigervnc died, systemd restarted, repeat; websockify on the same host answered noVNC with close code 1011 ("Failed to connect to downstream server"). The vnc.service unit now runs an `ExecStartPre` (as root, via systemd's `+` prefix) that re-chowns `/var/lib/vpt_user`, `~/.cache` and `~/.local/share` to vpt_user's UID/GID, logs `🔧 VPT-VNC AUTO-FIX: $d was [cur], repaired to vpt_user (UID:GID)` to the journal whenever it had to fix something (greppable with `journalctl -u vpt-vnc | grep AUTO-FIX`), and prints a 5-line FATAL banner with the original state and the actual `chown`/`install -d` error before `exit 1` if the repair itself can't complete (read-only mount, full disk). In plain words: the crash loop self-heals and now leaves a breadcrumb; an unrecoverable error says so on the spot instead of timing out 40 s of xauth then dying · [BUG-0435](../bugs/BUG-0435-2026-10-07-vpt-vnc-silently-crash-loops-when-vpt_user-home-is-root-owned.md)
- **BLE remote button overlays land in place the moment the panel swaps to the remote image** — when the action screen ("Start Pairing / Preparing the remote") transitioned to the visual BLE remote, the clickable button overlays were drawn for one frame with the `calculateRemoteScale()` fallback (a `window.innerHeight` / stream-height heuristic) and only snapped to the real flex/aspect-ratio-driven height *after* paint, so the user saw a ~150 ms "buttons slide into place" animation. With `useEffect` → `useLayoutEffect` on the image-box measurement (`BluetoothRemote.tsx`), the measurement + state commit land before the browser paints, so the first frame is already at the correct scale — no flash, no transition. In plain words: after a successful pair, the buttons are where they should be on the very first frame · [BUG-0436](../bugs/BUG-0436-2026-10-08-ble-remote-button-overlays-misplaced-on-success.md)
- **IR remote works on a non-Pi host (Minisforum)** — a USB IR Toy plugged into an x86 server showed up in `lsusb` but `ls /dev/lirc*` was always empty, so the STB panel did nothing. Not a missing package: the stock Debian kernel builds no `ir_toy` driver, so nothing ever claims that dongle for IR and the host's `ir-ctl` has no device to write to. A third transport writes the waveform straight to the dongle's serial port instead, using the same key codes, chosen with `DEVICEn_IR_SERIAL=/dev/irtoy` — the installer detects the platform and sets up the `/dev/irtoy` name and permissions for you. No kernel change, and nothing moves on a Pi. In plain words: the same IR dongle now works on the Minisforum, configured like a Pi · [BUG-0434](../bugs/BUG-0434-2026-10-07-no-ir-on-the-minisforum-lirc-has-no-usable-node.md)
- **Two servers sharing a name no longer look like the same row** — the picker now appends the host (`Shared — vpt-pi1.example`) when two servers answer to one `SERVER_NAME`, and only then, so a server with a name of its own is unchanged. The sign-in dialog says the same. In plain words: if two servers share a name, you can still tell them apart · [BUG-0432](../bugs/BUG-0432-2026-10-07-two-servers-with-one-name-look-alike.md)
- **A dropped TLS session no longer costs a background loop a whole cycle** — the Supabase path is reached through the main server, so a request could fail for reasons that have nothing to do with the query (a TLS session dropping mid-flight, or a proxy answering 500 while the far end comes up). Those are now retried in place and logged in one readable line; a real API error still surfaces on the first attempt. In plain words: a momentary network hiccup no longer skips an analytics refresh or a report purge · [BUG-0433](../bugs/BUG-0433-2026-10-07-a-dropped-tls-session-cost-a-cycle.md)
- **A server listed twice no longer shows up twice** — repeating a server in the configuration (the usual way: the same URL also in the slave list) gave it two identical rows in the picker and sent it two identical requests on every refresh. `<url>`, `<url>/` and `<url>/server` are now recognised as the same server, and the first spelling wins. In plain words: a URL you list twice is one server, and it is asked once · [BUG-0431](../bugs/BUG-0431-2026-10-07-a-server-listed-twice-gave-two-rows.md)
- **The server picker listed the primary server's name on every row** — a fix meant to stop `team_id` piling up in URLs was also dropping the origin, so every request aimed at another server went to the one serving the page. The picker showed the primary's name once per configured server, and picking a different server still gave you the primary's devices, workspaces and reports. In plain words: switching servers did not switch servers · [BUG-0430](../bugs/BUG-0430-2026-10-07-server-picker-shows-the-primary-server-name.md)
- **Switch controller could grab the STB remote's Bluetooth adapter** — vpt-procon now stays on its own adapter, so one host can drive a Switch (USB dongle) and a BLE STB remote (onboard) at once. In plain words: a Pi can now control a Nintendo Switch and an STB together · [BUG-0429](../bugs/BUG-0429-2026-10-06-procon-takes-first-bluetooth-adapter.md) · `a13b056b2`
- **AI comment dialog showed "Manual Review" for automatic verdicts** — it now says Jev (automatic), AI model (automatic), skipped or failed · [BUG-0428](../bugs/BUG-0428-2026-10-06-ai-comment-dialog-called-automatic-verdicts-manual-review.md)
- **Audio-loss alerts show a screenshot again, and only once** — vpt-transcript opened its own audio-loss alert next to vpt-monitor's and could never attach a frame to it; it no longer opens alerts. An audio loss re-opened after a black screen now takes the current frame. In plain words: one audio-loss alert per loss, with a picture · [BUG-0427](../bugs/BUG-0427-2026-10-06-audio-loss-alert-without-screenshot.md)
- **Heatmap: incidents badge filters KO, steady analysis table** — clicking "Incidents detected" toggles the KO view and back to All; the Data Analysis table keeps fixed column widths instead of jumping with each device name; the capture popup has the gold border and a bigger close button. In plain words: the heatmap page stops flickering and the badge is a shortcut
- **Test reports follow the light / dark choice** — reports declare that they handle dark mode themselves (browsers stopped auto-darkening the light theme) and share the app's theme setting, so switching in the app switches open reports too ([BUG-0426](../bugs/BUG-0426-2026-10-05-report-theme-stays-dark.md)). In plain words: picking light shows a light report.
- **BLE remote: pairing waits 2 minutes instead of 1** — the panel gave up after 60 s while the STB often needs up to ~2 min to pair, so a pairing that was still succeeding showed as failed. In plain words: no more false "pairing failed" and needless retries · [BUG-0425](../bugs/BUG-0425-2026-10-05-ble-pairing-panel-times-out-before-stb.md)
- **Take control records the logged-in user, not the default user** — device locks and the take-control history now store the account the server verified at login (with its email), instead of the id the browser sends, which was the built-in default user for some logged-in browsers. In plain words: you can see who took control of a device · [BUG-0424](../bugs/BUG-0424-2026-10-05-take-control-records-default-user.md)
- **Run Tests lists the subtitle check's arguments** — picking `subtitle_qa` showed "No parameters" because its argument list sat past the first 300 lines the form reads; it is now at the top of the script. In plain words: the subtitle check can be set up and started from the Run Tests page · [BUG-0423](../bugs/BUG-0423-2026-10-05-subtitle-qa-run-tests-form-no-parameters.md) · `b3360ce34`
- **BLE remote: every Start Pairing names who ran it** — each run is written to `/var/log/vpt-ble-pairing.log` on the host with the user, role, IP, adapter and the link state at the click (`healthy` = a working remote was wiped, `no_keys` = connected but keys were dropped, `no_link` = no STB); the BLE diagnostic collector prints it. Pairing itself is unchanged. In plain words: when a remote is wiped we can see who did it · [BUG-0422](../bugs/BUG-0422-2026-10-05-ble-start-pairing-wipes-working-remotes.md)
- **Grafana page: no more 403 on first open, and only dashboards you can open are listed** — the page opened on Server Monitoring, which customer viewers cannot read ("Failed to load dashboard", 403), and listed FullZap Results for everyone. It now opens on Script Results, drops FullZap, and asks Grafana which dashboards the viewer may open and lists only those (the full list stays if Grafana cannot be reached from the browser). In plain words: customers no longer pick a dashboard that just shows an error · [BUG-0421](../bugs/BUG-0421-2026-10-05-grafana-page-lists-dashboards-viewer-cannot-open.md)
- **BLE remote: "Waking STB…" shown once while waking** — the action row no longer adds its own spinner and "Waking STB…" label next to the disabled Power button's "Waking…"; the heading and detail line state the wake once, Re-pair and Logs stay. In plain words: one wake indicator instead of three · [BUG-0420](../bugs/BUG-0420-2026-10-05-ble-remote-waking-shown-twice.md)
- **Test case run counts load again** — listing test cases asked the database for the runs of all 201 test cases in one request whose URL was over 10 KB, so the gateway answered "414 URI too long" and every test case came back with 0 runs (the main server logged it on every list). Reads that pass a list of ids or names now ask 50 at a time everywhere (test cases, campaigns, tags, navigation trees and metrics, campaign results, report labels). Server deploy. In plain words: test case and campaign run counts are right again, and big teams no longer trip the URL limit · [BUG-0417](../bugs/BUG-0417-2026-10-05-testcase-execution-stats-414-uri-too-long.md)
- **A web host can show the Localize button** — set `HOST_USERINTERFACE` in the host `.env` and the Device page offers Localize for the browser device, as it already did for numbered devices · [BUG-0419](../bugs/BUG-0419-2026-10-05-web-host-device-no-localize-button.md)
- **Test Reports loads again on the RPI1 server** — the run-label lookup added by TASK-42 asked the database for 100 runs at once; the answer echoes that whole request in a header, and past ~4 KB the public proxy refused it, so the page showed Cloudflare's "502 Bad gateway" HTML. It now asks 50 at a time, and the page shows a short error instead of a raw HTML dump. In plain words: the reports table on the Pi server is back, and a database hiccup no longer fills the page with code · [BUG-0416](../bugs/BUG-0416-2026-10-05-test-reports-502-on-rpi1-server-long-label-lookup.md)
- **Localize names the right page of a website** — all pages share one header, so it listed every page as a candidate; it now compares the two best regions. In plain words: the home page of a web map reads "Localized: home", not a list of six · [BUG-0418](../bugs/BUG-0418-2026-10-05-localize-website-pages-tie-on-shared-header.md)
- **Reloading a page no longer sends you to the login page, and Save says why it did not save** — a tab signed in with `?auto_signed=<token>` lost that sign-in on reload (production builds have auto-sign disabled, so the stored token was wiped); it is now kept for the tab. For a normal login, the app gave up on restoring the session after 3 s and showed login while the session was still being refreshed (an expired token, and every startup request queued on the same session); it now waits up to 35 s, and the login page sends you back to the page you were on once the session is there. Separately, a rejected Virtual Scripts save (syntax error, name already used) or a network failure showed nothing and left "Unsaved", so it looked like the click had not registered; the page now shows the syntax error with its line or the server's reason. In plain words: F5 keeps you where you were, and when Save fails you see why · [BUG-0392](../bugs/BUG-0392-2026-10-05-virtual-scripts-page-login-on-reload-silent-save-test-interfaces.md)
- **Node screenshots save their Localize fingerprint again** — the host read the frame after the upload had deleted it, so no node got a fingerprint; the MCP `save_node_screenshot` also dropped it. In plain words: Localize now works on nodes whose screenshots were captured through the product · [BUG-0415](../bugs/BUG-0415-2026-10-05-node-screenshot-saved-no-fingerprint.md)
- **A host's AI call no longer crashes while the server is unreachable** — it returns "AI gateway unreachable", so callers can back off; it used to raise `UnboundLocalError` (TASK-05 timeout budget). In plain words: a server restart no longer breaks host-side AI features mid-step · [BUG-0412](../bugs/BUG-0412-2026-10-05-host-ai-call-crashed-when-the-server-was-unreachable.md)
- **Text checks score on word boundaries** — a short target such as "Home" no longer passes because the OCR text contains it inside a longer word ("Aufnahmen"); one-character OCR misreads still pass. In plain words: fewer false "text found" results on borderline checks · [BUG-0406](../bugs/BUG-0406-2026-10-05-text-checks-passed-on-a-short-word-hidden-inside-a-longer-one.md)
- **Agents no longer take a device someone else is using, and are told who has it** — `scripts/atlas_chat.py --host/--device` took the device with the force-takeover path (which preempts another session's control and stops a running script); it now takes control like the web UI and stops when the device is held. The MCP `take_control` tool no longer stops a running script unless asked with `allow_takeover=true`. Atlas and the MCP device tools (actions, navigation, test cases) now run only under the caller's own control — the chat's own take-control, its browser tab's, or the same signed-in user's — and otherwise refuse; a refusal used to say only "Device … is locked" and now names who holds it ("is in use by <user> (<kind of control>). Not taking it over…"), like the other device tools. `force_unlock` no longer clears another person's control. A device driven without taking control (directly over adb, vpt-lease only) is still invisible to the lock. In plain words: Atlas test chats wait their turn instead of grabbing the phone, Atlas never drives a device someone else has taken, and an agent told no is also told by whom · [BUG-0402](../bugs/BUG-0402-2026-10-05-atlas-chat-took-a-phone-another-agent-was-using.md)
- **"My selected devices" in Atlas means the chat's Target, every time** — phrases like "my selected devices", "this device" or "the target" are detected in your message and the analytics tools scope the query, chart, export or Grafana draft to the Target device (named in the reply: "Limited to your selected device ...") instead of relying on the model to ask for it; devices you name still win, and a Target on another server is reported as not on this server · [BUG-0405](../bugs/BUG-0405-2026-10-05-atlas-my-selected-devices-ignored-the-chat-target.md)
- **MCP navigation returns the screen it reached** — `navigate_to_node` (and the action and verification tools with `include_screenshot`) never delivered their screenshot: the server fetched it through a public path it cannot reach, so the model said the image "did not come back". It is now read from the host, and `navigate_to_node` and `verify_node` include it by default. In plain words: an AI agent that opens a screen over MCP sees it · [BUG-0413](../bugs/BUG-0413-2026-10-05-mcp-inline-screenshot-never-returned.md)
- **MCP `verify_node` accepts a node name or a node id** — the schema demanded the name even though the id was accepted, so a call with the id was refused. In plain words: one fewer retry for the agent · [BUG-0414](../bugs/BUG-0414-2026-10-05-mcp-verify-node-schema-required-node-label.md)
- **MCP navigate_to_node no longer restarts from the entry screen or times out** — the navigation route told the host the position was unknown on every call, so each run relaunched the app and waited (about 34 s for one hop) and the MCP call gave up after 60 s while the page was already loaded. The host now keeps its tracked position, and the MCP wait is 120 s. In plain words: an AI agent asking to open a screen gets the result in seconds, not a timeout · [BUG-0411](../bugs/BUG-0411-2026-10-05-mcp-navigate-to-node-restarted-from-entry-and-timed-out.md)
- **A Grafana draft from "my selected devices" opens with the device selected** — the Host and Device dropdowns now show the Target (like a device you name) instead of All, so you can widen to every device; panels you scoped individually stay fixed · [BUG-0410](../bugs/BUG-0410-2026-10-05-grafana-draft-from-selected-device-opened-with-host-and-device-on-all.md)
- **Agent Chat no longer hangs silently when the server is half-dead** — a send during a server restart that accepts connections but never answers sat there with no feedback; it now shows "Reconnecting…" within 3 seconds, retries for up to a minute, then says plainly that the message was not sent (your text stays in the box). A message re-sent after a lost acknowledgement is processed once. [BUG-0401](../bugs/BUG-0401-2026-10-05-agent-chat-send-hung-forever-when-the-server-accepts-but-never-answers.md)
- **Grafana drafts keep preset defaults inside their panel** — a KPI preset's default environments and the defaulted interface were turned into dashboard-wide variable selections, so the other panels no longer matched the chat canvas (pass rate 67.7% instead of 72.7%). Only filters you asked for, named devices and the current device become variable selections now; defaults stay fixed in their own panel (its description says so) and every variable stays on All. In plain words: the Grafana dashboard shows the same numbers as the chat canvas · [BUG-0396](../bugs/BUG-0396-2026-10-05-grafana-draft-turned-preset-defaults-into-dashboard-filters.md)
- **Go To returns the Run button as soon as the device arrives** — the panel waited up to 2 minutes for a socket event before reading the navigation's status, so a missed event left it on "Executing…" long after the phone or box had arrived. It now watches both from the start, treats aborted as finished and stops with an error when the host no longer knows the run. In plain words: Go To no longer hangs after it has actually finished · [BUG-0395](../bugs/BUG-0395-2026-10-05-go-to-panel-stayed-on-executing-after-the-device-arrived.md)
- **Virtual script reports carry the virtual script's name** — a run of `smoke_goto_live` was reported as `goto_live.py`, the `@script()` name typed inside its code; the report, result row and artefacts now use the virtual script's own name (disk scripts unchanged). In plain words: the report is named after the script you ran · [BUG-0391](../bugs/BUG-0391-2026-10-05-virtual-script-report-named-after-its-script-decorator.md)
- **MCP screenshots carry the image again** — `capture_screenshot` fetched the picture from an nginx on the server VM that production does not have, so every answer came without the image and without OCR. It now fetches it from the host that holds it, through `call_host()`. In plain words: an AI agent using the MCP tools can see the device screen · [BUG-0388](../bugs/BUG-0388-2026-10-05-mcp-capture-screenshot-never-returned-the-image.md)
- **MCP navigate_to_node waits for a cold navigation** — the first navigation after a restart (cache to build, 20–93 s) answered "Request timeout (30s)" while it carried on and reached the screen. The tool now waits up to 180 s, the navigation route's own limit; on its own this was not enough, the hang was only gone with BUG-0411 (same deploy). In plain words: the first "go to screen X" no longer reports a failure it did not have · [BUG-0389](../bugs/BUG-0389-2026-10-05-mcp-navigate-to-node-timed-out-on-a-cold-navigation.md)
- **Build Campaign shows a script's parameters again** — opening a script in the run order called an analyze URL the server does not have and stayed on "Loading parameters…"; it now uses the same request as Run Tests, and a failure shows its reason instead of loading forever. In plain words: you can set a script's arguments in a campaign again · [BUG-0386](../bugs/BUG-0386-2026-10-05-build-campaign-script-parameters-stuck-on-loading.md)
- **Device page keeps your saved Target filter** — on reload the saved Target (and Flag) selection was cleared because the filters were checked against the device list before it had loaded. In plain words: the Device page reopens on the targets you picked · [BUG-0387](../bugs/BUG-0387-2026-10-05-device-page-dropped-saved-target-filter-on-load.md)
- **Script logs no longer print device-farm keys** — the start of every execution log listed each `DEVICE*` environment variable with its value, including cloud-farm keys; it now lists the names only. In plain words: report logs no longer reveal passwords · [BUG-0393](../bugs/BUG-0393-2026-10-05-script-logs-printed-device-farm-keys.md)
- **Reports no longer show the caller's IP** — the `Trigger:` line of script and testcase reports dropped `ip=…`; the IP is still kept in the database for audit. In plain words: sharing a report no longer shares your IP address · [BUG-0394](../bugs/BUG-0394-2026-10-05-reports-showed-the-callers-ip-address.md)
- **Generated Grafana charts no longer mix % with counts or overlap their labels** — a bar chart of pass rate per device also plotted the run count on the percent axis (730 runs read "730%"); now each column keeps its own unit, the chart plots the pass rate and the run count stays in the tooltip, long device names are rotated and shortened, and stat tiles stop turning red above 80. [BUG-0390](../bugs/BUG-0390-2026-10-05-generated-grafana-charts-mixed-percent-and-counts-and-overlapping-labels.md)
- **Generated Grafana dashboards show data** — every panel of an Atlas-generated dashboard failed with `column "__all" does not exist` while its filters were on All; the template's All value is now quoted · [BUG-0385](../bugs/BUG-0385-2026-10-05-generated-grafana-dashboards-failed-column-all-does-not-exist.md)
- **Atlas no longer invents a host for a Grafana link** — a client that does not send the app's Grafana address now gets the dashboard path with an instruction to repeat it as written, instead of a made-up domain · [BUG-0384](../bugs/BUG-0384-2026-10-05-atlas-invented-a-host-for-a-relative-grafana-link.md)
- **Atlas Grafana links open the right Grafana** — the "Open in Grafana" link on a canvas and the link in a dashboard answer pointed at the marketing website (404) because the server built them from `GRAFANA_DOMAIN`. The server now returns a path and the web app resolves it against its own Grafana address (`VITE_GRAFANA_URL`), the same one the Monitoring links use; the chat sends that address so the answer's link is absolute, also from the Pi-hosted server. No new env keys. In plain words: the Grafana link in an Atlas answer now opens · [BUG-0381](../bugs/BUG-0381-2026-10-05-atlas-grafana-draft-links-opened-a-404-on-the-marketing-webs.md)
- **Grafana dashboards from Atlas no longer claim filters you did not ask for** — the answer says "Preselected filters: none, every variable starts on All" unless you named a device or filter, and a device you name in the request is now applied to the dashboard like it is to charts. In plain words: "pass rate by device" shows all your devices · [BUG-0382](../bugs/BUG-0382-2026-10-05-pass-rate-by-device-grafana-dashboard-answers-claimed-filter.md)
- **Atlas post-deploy suite records every check and fails on a dead link** — checks with no measurable result were silently dropped; the dashboard check now judges the outcome (one draft with 2+ panels and a link that returns 200, with or without the word "grafana"), links are fetched and fail on 404, a row-count self-check guards against lost rows, and the new-chat outage check no longer crashes when the page opens on an old conversation. In plain words: the live check can no longer miss a broken Grafana link · [BUG-0383](../bugs/BUG-0383-2026-10-05-atlas-post-deploy-suite-lost-two-checks-and-could-not-catch.md)
- **Atlas chat runs had times 2 hours in the future** — the server stored its local Zurich time as UTC; the Agent Dashboard showed chat activity in the future and out of order · [BUG-0377](../bugs/BUG-0377-2026-10-05-chat-run-times-stored-2-hours-in-the-future.md)
- **MCP `execute_testcase` and `execute_script` return at once; a client behind a proxy no longer gets a 524** — both tools used to wait for the end of the run inside one request (up to 5 minutes for a testcase, 2 hours for a script), which a proxy cuts off after 100 s. They now return an `execution_id`; `get_testcase_execution_status` / `get_script_execution_status` read the run, each call answering within 50 s. **MCP clients must call the status tool after starting a run** ([BUG-0378](../bugs/BUG-0378-2026-10-05-execute-testcase-held-one-request-open-for-the-whole-run.md)).
- **QuickTest's test list is there straight after a refresh** — the saved-tests rail is kept in the browser and refreshed in the background instead of starting empty each time; the rail's duplicate "Quick tests" heading is gone and search sits next to + on one row. In plain words: reload the page and your quick tests are already listed · [BUG-0372](../bugs/BUG-0372-2026-10-05-quicktest-rail-reloads-on-every-refresh.md)
- **Agent Dashboard no longer shows fake data** — the leaderboard is per team (a CI test agent was ranked first), a benchmark run no longer reports a made-up 100 %, agent status and version are the real ones, and the tabs scroll on a phone · [BUG-0371](../bugs/BUG-0371-2026-10-05-the-agent-dashboard-showed-fake-agent-data.md)
- **CI no longer dies on a full disk** — the self-hosted test runners never cleaned up after their jobs, so one VM filled its disk and nearly every regression run failed while installing dependencies. Each runner VM now rotates job workspaces, logs, caches and unused Docker images (3-day retention, daily plus a check every 15 minutes) and skips any runner that is running a job · [BUG-0367](../bugs/BUG-0367-2026-10-05-ci-runner-disks-fill-up-and-every-regression-run-fails.md)
- **Regression Tests is green again on main** — three leftovers behind the full-disk failures: a lint error (an unused eslint-disable in the agent chat page, [BUG-0369](../bugs/BUG-0369-2026-10-05-frontend-lint-failed-on-an-unused-eslintdisable.md)), two broken file paths in the standalone install guide ([BUG-0368](../bugs/BUG-0368-2026-10-05-the-docs-path-check-failed-on-two.md)), and a Docker-stack pre-pull that tried to download images that are only built locally ([BUG-0370](../bugs/BUG-0370-2026-10-05-the-docker-stack-job-wasted-90-s.md)).
- **A failing lint or component test now turns the GitHub job red** — both were recorded as failed in the CI database while GitHub showed a green job, which hid a lint error and three broken page tests; the tests are repaired and the jobs now fail honestly ([BUG-0374](../bugs/BUG-0374-2026-10-05-failing-lint-and-component-tests-showed-as.md)). The shared CI runner with the small disk also prunes unused Docker data while jobs run ([BUG-0375](../bugs/BUG-0375-2026-10-05-runner-vm-163-root-disk-31-gb.md)).
- **A new push now replaces the older waiting test run of the same branch (a running one always finishes)** — every push used to queue a complete run, so results for the newest commit waited hours behind runs of commits already replaced ([BUG-0376](../bugs/BUG-0376-2026-10-05-every-push-queued-a-full-regression-run.md)).
- **The browser (E2E) test jobs no longer collide when installing the browser's system packages** — jobs sharing a runner VM fought over the package manager lock and some E2E jobs never started the browser ([BUG-0373](../bugs/BUG-0373-2026-10-05-e2e-jobs-fail-installing-playwright-browser-deps-apt-lock.md)).
- **Device Control no longer breaks after a frontend deploy** — the proxy marked error responses as cacheable for a year, so a chunk requested during the deploy's restart stayed a 500 at Cloudflare; errors are no longer cached (proxy config) · [BUG-0365](../bugs/BUG-0365-2026-10-05-cloudflare-cached-chunk-500-for-a-year.md)
- **The top bar stays still** — items no longer jump sideways when you click one, the spacing between them is even, and the current page is readable in light mode (marked in gold instead of pink on blue), the dropdown menus keep one width in every language, and the Theme / Language choice in Settings is marked in the same gold · [BUG-0366](../bugs/BUG-0366-2026-10-05-navbar-items-shift-on-navigation.md)
- **Test Prompt follows light mode** — the page stayed dark when the app was set to light; its sidebar, header and form now use a light palette there, dark mode unchanged · [BUG-0364](../bugs/BUG-0364-2026-10-05-test-prompt-dark-in-light-mode.md)
- **A generated test no longer passes without the steps the AI repeated** — Run graph dropped every step line it could not read, so "zap 3 times and check" ran as just "go to live" and passed. A repeat is now written out, and a step line or command the builder cannot use fails the generation with that error · [BUG-0363](../bugs/BUG-0363-2026-10-04-generated-graph-dropped-loop-steps.md)
- **Anonymous requests could reach the host proxy through an unauthenticated path prefix** — `/server/health/…`, `/server/share/…` and the other exempt prefixes now exempt only their own routes; anything falling through to the catch-all needs auth, and dot segments are refused · [BUG-0362](../bugs/BUG-0362-2026-10-04-unauthenticated-prefix-reached-auto-proxy.md)
- **The Switch controller daemon restarts itself instead of staying dead** — on vpt-pi1 nxbt's manager ran out of file descriptors after repeated controller re-creations and the daemon retried against it for 2 hours; it now exits (systemd restarts it) when the manager is dead, leaking descriptors, or 5 creations in a row fail. In plain words: if the Switch link wedges, it heals itself within seconds instead of needing a manual restart · [BUG-0361](../bugs/BUG-0361-2026-10-04-procon-daemon-wedged-fd-leak.md)
- **Saving a passing exploration keeps every step** — "zap 3 times" was saved with one zap and AI-invented checks; every step is kept now and the agent's own screen checks are saved, with no LLM call · [BUG-0356](../bugs/BUG-0356-2026-10-04-saved-exploration-lost-repeated-steps.md) · `065b8ae9df`
- **"Wait for image to disappear" passes once the image is gone** — a black frame while a channel tuned failed `home → live` on stb4; the check now passes at the first frame without the image (and its KPI works) · [BUG-0357](../bugs/BUG-0357-2026-10-04-wait-for-image-to-disappear-any-frame.md) · `065b8ae9df`
- **A failed IR key press says why** — instead of "no error details from controller" · [BUG-0358](../bugs/BUG-0358-2026-10-04-ir-key-press-failure-no-reason.md) · `065b8ae9df`
- **New AI Agent chat shows "Reconnecting to the server…"** — the landing view now shows the notice and send errors like the thread view · [BUG-0360](../bugs/BUG-0360-2026-10-04-agentchat-landing-no-reconnect-notice.md) · `d5450d284`
- **Agent Chat recovers by itself after a server restart** — opening the page while the server is down now shows "Reconnecting to the server…" and retries (1 s doubling to 15 s) instead of staying broken until a refresh [BUG-0350](../bugs/BUG-0350-2026-10-04-agent-chat-health-check-no-retry.md)
- **Atlas answers "no data" at once for an empty KPI comparison** — an empty KPI result now says which interfaces and variants do have samples in scope (or none), and every empty reply tells the model to answer without more queries, instead of 12 guessed queries over 46 s · [BUG-0345](../bugs/BUG-0345-2026-10-04-atlas-kpi-no-data-thrash.md)
- **Atlas enforces the devices you name** — devices named in the message are detected and applied as the filter (or reported as not on this server, with no other devices charted), and titles never name devices outside the filter · [BUG-0355](../bugs/BUG-0355-2026-10-04-atlas-named-devices-not-enforced.md)
- **An Android TV with an ADB serial kept its HDMI video** — `DEVICE<N>_VIDEO` now wins over `DEVICE<N>_ADB_SERIAL` in `run_ffmpeg.sh`, and the scrcpy grabber gets its real serial instead of failing with "Unknown source type for ADB". Takes effect at the next `vpt-stream` restart · [BUG-0344](../bugs/BUG-0344-2026-10-04-adb-serial-steals-hdmi-capture.md) · `2c1347446`
- **Manager alerts now show in the dashboard** — a bell with an unread count and an alerts sheet (refreshed every 30 s) list what the AI manager and ghosty sent, which only reached Web Push before; a re-fired stop is no longer appended to the manager's event file again within 6 h · [BUG-0343](../bugs/BUG-0343-2026-10-04-manager-alerts-never-shown-in-dashboard.md)
- **A deploy runner restart no longer leaves a deploy stuck as running** — the runner now finishes a running deploy it lost track of as failed once its log is idle for 10 minutes, so the queue moves on; a running deploy now also survives a runner restart (detached child, re-adopted on start) · [BUG-0341](../bugs/BUG-0341-2026-10-04-deploy-runner-orphaned-running-deploy-after-restart.md)
- **Full deploys no longer report a failure for the switched-off labox-tablet** — the host is removed from the deploy host list · [BUG-0342](../bugs/BUG-0342-2026-10-04-full-deploy-false-failure-for-offline-labox-tablet.md)
- **Audio no longer reported missing on streams that have sound** — the check now measures the whole segment's peak instead of the first half-second's average, and a probe that times out keeps the last state · [BUG-0315](../bugs/BUG-0315-2026-10-04-audio-check-false-no-audio.md) · `6386679964` `4d9853e009`
- **Atlas no longer picks a device from another server as 'my device'** — when the chat's device belongs to a different server than the selected one, analytics uses the visible fleet instead of failing first · [BUG-0311](../bugs/BUG-0311-2026-10-04-atlas-foreign-device-current-device-scope.md)
- **Atlas time charts aggregate when no grain is given — a trend over time now buckets by hour (range up to 2 days) or day instead of drawing one raw row per event; the chosen grain is shown** · [BUG-0320](../bugs/BUG-0320-2026-10-04-atlas-time-series-no-grain-raw-rows.md)
- **Atlas comparisons no longer fail on an odd direction — the metric decides the direction and the result notes the override** · [BUG-0321](../bugs/BUG-0321-2026-10-04-atlas-comparison-bad-direction-errors.md)
- **Atlas source errors list the valid sources — an empty or misspelt source id gets the valid ids and close matches, and a canvas names the panel at fault** · [BUG-0322](../bugs/BUG-0322-2026-10-04-atlas-unknown-source-error-no-valid-ids.md)
- **KPI charts no longer fail when you do not name an interface — Atlas uses the conversation's interface, else the one with the most KPI samples, and says which** · [BUG-0330](../bugs/BUG-0330-2026-10-04-atlas-kpi-preset-missing-interface.md)
- **Atlas is told never to swap the devices you name — it must say none matched and offer the closest names, not chart other devices** · [BUG-0331](../bugs/BUG-0331-2026-10-04-atlas-prompt-substitutes-named-devices.md)
- **Atlas charts requested together now land in one canvas — separate chart calls in one answer are merged instead of the last one hiding the rest** · [BUG-0335](../bugs/BUG-0335-2026-10-04-atlas-canvas-split-into-single-panel-calls.md)
- **Agent Chat no longer loses a message sent while the server restarts — it shows "Reconnecting…", delivers once the server is back (up to 60 s), and otherwise says it was not sent and keeps your text** · [BUG-0340](../bugs/BUG-0340-2026-10-04-agent-chat-send-lost-during-server-restart.md)
- **Atlas says so when a device you named does not exist — filters on device/host that match nothing are reported to the model with 'do not substitute other devices', instead of silently charting other devices** · [BUG-0323](../bugs/BUG-0323-2026-10-04-atlas-silent-device-substitution.md)
- **Analytics field-name errors list the valid fields** — a wrong dimension/metric now gets a close-match hint and the source's valid fields, so one retry works · [BUG-0312](../bugs/BUG-0312-2026-10-04-atlas-unsupported-fields-error-no-valid-list.md)
- **Analytics tables scroll sideways in canvas cells instead of cutting columns** — headers stay on one line and every column is reachable · [BUG-0317](../bugs/BUG-0317-2026-10-04-analytics-table-cut-off-in-canvas-cell.md) · `3fd687987a`
- **Analytics charts show durations as s / min / h and no longer clip the Y axis** — 1,800,000 ms reads "30 min"; CSV/JSON exports keep raw numbers · [BUG-0316](../bugs/BUG-0316-2026-10-04-analytics-large-durations-overflow-axis.md) · `3fd687987a`
- **Agent chat drops a Target from another server when you switch servers** — the toolbar Target and the host sent with each message now always belong to the selected server · [BUG-0313](../bugs/BUG-0313-2026-10-04-agent-chat-target-survives-server-switch.md) · `c92efe050e`
- **Analytics bar chart labels no longer cover the legend** — long category labels are shortened on the axis (full value in the tooltip) and the axis reserves room for them · [BUG-0314](../bugs/BUG-0314-2026-10-04-analytics-bar-labels-overlap-legend.md) · `c92efe050e`
- **Live player no longer 404s after a host renames its stream folders** — the server kept each device's stream URL for 24 h; a host re-registering now clears its cached URLs · [BUG-0308](../bugs/BUG-0308-2026-10-04-stream-url-cache-survives-host-reregistration.md) · `d5cc241570`
- **Analytics canvas shows the scope note once** — a multi-panel answer repeated "Limited to N host(s)…" in every panel; it now appears once for the canvas and once in the report export · [BUG-0305](../bugs/BUG-0305-2026-10-04-canvas-scope-note-repeated-per-panel.md)
- **Comparison radar is visible without scrolling** — the radar now sits beside the comparison table (above it on narrow panels) instead of below it · [BUG-0306](../bugs/BUG-0306-2026-10-04-comparison-radar-below-the-table.md)
- **Chat clears the error banner of a recovered tool call** — when a tool failed but the agent went on and answered, the red banner stayed at the bottom; it is now cleared once the turn recovers (real failures still show) · [BUG-0307](../bugs/BUG-0307-2026-10-04-agent-chat-stale-tool-error-banner.md)
- **Atlas builds a canvas instead of asking which devices** — it now knows the selected device by name, treats "my devices" as the visible fleet when none is selected, says the scope it used, and follows tool-error hints instead of asking · [BUG-0303](../bugs/BUG-0303-2026-10-04-atlas-canvas-asked-instead-of-building.md)
- **Stream previews keep the device you picked** — after switching devices, a slow answer for the previous device could replace the stream, so the preview showed another device's picture (seen on Test Prompt: stb4's stream during a phone run); late answers for another device are now ignored · [BUG-0301](../bugs/BUG-0301-2026-10-04-stream-preview-showed-another-devices-stream.md) · `this commit`
- **An Atlas chat started during a Test Prompt run crashed** — it now waits its turn and says so · [BUG-0300](../bugs/BUG-0300-2026-10-04-atlas-chat-crashed-while-a-test-prompt-ran.md) · `04cd29529`
- **Hosts no longer push alerts to a Redis queue nobody reads** — alert triage (`vpt-discard-incidents`) now reads the alerts table itself, with a cheap vision model and cost rules (first alert per device+type per 6 h, ≤100 calls/day); it stays disabled: the evaluation caught only 3 of 6 real incidents. A host deploy must restart `vpt-monitor` · [BUG-0299](../bugs/BUG-0299-2026-10-04-alerts-queued-to-redis-with-no-consumer.md)
- **A grabber showing a black or frozen picture is archived again** — the archiver merged each minute with a probe per segment, and a static picture makes 0.4 s segments too small (~1.5 KB) for ffmpeg to recognise, so every minute failed and was lost (a sleeping Nintendo Switch on vpt-pi1 archived nothing for 19 h). A minute is now read as one MPEG-TS stream. A host deploy must restart `vpt-archiver` · [BUG-0298](../bugs/BUG-0298-2026-10-04-archiver-failed-every-minute-on-a-black-or-static-picture.md) · `this commit`
- **HDMI captures keep 4 minutes of segments for the archiver instead of 2** — a finished minute now survives ~3-4 min in hot storage before ffmpeg deletes it, so an archiver cycle running 2+ minutes late no longer loses part of it; the live playlist grows from 60 s to 120 s (300 segments) and the extra RAM (~25 MB per device) is only used while the archiver is behind. Takes effect at the next `vpt-stream` restart; restart `vpt-archiver` with it · [BUG-0297](../bugs/BUG-0297-2026-10-04-hot-kept-only-two-minutes-of-live-segments-for-the-archiver.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: long runs are no longer called timed out** — the page gave up after 3 minutes and showed *Execution timed out* while the run went on and passed; it now waits for the run's real result · [BUG-0294](../bugs/BUG-0294-2026-10-04-test-prompt-page-gave-up-on-runs-over-3-min.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: a name already in use says so** — Run silently did nothing; it now says the prompt name exists and to open it or pick another · [BUG-0295](../bugs/BUG-0295-2026-10-04-test-prompt-duplicate-name-run-did-nothing.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: Save as testcase only after a passing graph run** · [BUG-0296](../bugs/BUG-0296-2026-10-04-test-prompt-failed-graph-run-offered-save.md) · `this commit`
- **MCP `get_transcript` returns the transcript, or why there is none** — last N minutes or an hour/10-min block of a device, with `status`/`reason`: transcription disabled on the host, service not running, no audio, silence, no speech, or a real fault. A host deploy must restart `vpt-transcript` · [BUG-0293](../bugs/BUG-0293-2026-10-04-mcp-get-transcript-never-returned-a-transcript.md)
- **Workspaces belong to the server they were made on** — servers sharing one database (Awesomation and vpt-pi1) no longer list each other's workspaces. Needs migration `20261004a_workspaces_server_name.sql` (`-v owner_server=...`) with the deploy; existing workspaces go to Awesomation; the same workspace name can be used on each server (migration `20261004b_workspaces_slug_per_server.sql`) · [BUG-0292](../bugs/BUG-0292-2026-10-04-every-server-on-a-shared-database-listed-every-workspace.md)
- **A busy host no longer drops whole minutes from the 24 h archive** — a late archiver cycle now archives every minute waiting in hot storage, oldest first, and a merge no longer fails when the stream writer deletes its oldest segment mid-merge; a minute that is still lost is named in the archiver log. On vpt-pi1 4-9 minutes per capture were lost in 2.5 h, leaving 540 s chunks. A host deploy must restart `vpt-archiver` · [BUG-0291](../bugs/BUG-0291-2026-10-04-a-late-archiver-cycle-lost-a-whole-minute.md) · `this commit`
- **Atlas ignored a bracketed `LOAD SKILL [name]` reply** — the parser now accepts the bracket the prompt asks for · [BUG-0290](../bugs/BUG-0290-2026-10-04-atlas-bracketed-load-skill-not-parsed.md) · `ff5f961b2`
- **Anthropic calls broke on the new SDK** — the client timeout is built with `anthropic.Timeout` · [BUG-0289](../bugs/BUG-0289-2026-10-04-anthropic-client-timeout-rejected-by-new-sdk.md) · `39c0f6a8e`
- **dns_lookuptime failed every hour** — a deployment passed the hostname as `--dns`; the script now rejects a non-IP `--dns` with a clear message · [BUG-0288](../bugs/BUG-0288-2026-10-04-dns-lookuptime-hostname-passed-as-dns-server.md) · `276cae858`
- **Targets dropdown: search box stays visible while scrolling** — it no longer slides behind the Select all / Clear row · [BUG-0287](../bugs/BUG-0287-2026-10-04-targets-dropdown-search-box-scrolled-out-of-view.md)
- **AI Generate on a phone: deleting a proposal no longer empties the plan** — the chip's delete rebuilt the list from TV-style rows, which a phone or web plan does not have, so Create Nodes/Edges made nothing; a click-based chip is now removed on its own · [BUG-0286](../bugs/BUG-0286-2026-10-04-ai-generate-create-made-nothing-after-a-chip-delete.md) · `this commit`
- **A stream restart no longer blanks the device's frames** — switching a source (`loadVideo` / `setUrl`), the SD/HD switch and watchdog restarts emptied the captures folder, and the new ffmpeg wrote nothing until the source's next keyframe (up to 26 s); a switch also killed every other device's ffmpeg. The last frames are now kept, numbering stays continuous, and the first new frame comes ~1.3–1.6 s after the switch · [BUG-0285](../bugs/BUG-0285-2026-10-04-stream-restart-emptied-captures-for-seconds.md) · `this commit`
- **Hosts logged "Added to analysis queue" for alerts Redis had refused** — the log now says the alert was not queued when Redis is unreachable · [BUG-0283](../bugs/BUG-0283-2026-10-04-alert-queue-log-said-added-after-redis-failed.md)
- **Charts and tables Atlas rendered never appeared in Agent Chat** — they now open in their own tab, with the export bar, readable dates and a clear empty state · [BUG-0282](../bugs/BUG-0282-2026-10-04-atlas-charts-never-appeared-in-agent-chat.md) · `9d2d590bc`, `1cfacae11`
- **Validate runs the entry edge** — the first step, Entry → home, was always skipped (*could not reposition to 'Entry'*: nothing leads into the entry point) and a tree whose every edge passed was reported failed; the entry edge now runs from wherever the device is · [BUG-0281](../bugs/BUG-0281-2026-10-04-validate-skipped-the-entry-edge.md) · `this commit`
- **AVQ icon in the stream modal closed the modal instead of opening AVQ** — feature links in the modal header now navigate, and Back returns to Device Control · [BUG-0280](../bugs/BUG-0280-2026-10-04-stream-modal-feature-links-closed-the-modal-instead-of-navigating.md)
- **AI Generate on Android checks home by its whole tab bar** — validation took Google TV's search page for home because it also says "Search", saved one BACK where two are needed, and the next item failed; home now means every validated item shows in the app's window · [BUG-0279](../bugs/BUG-0279-2026-10-04-ai-generate-took-the-search-page-for-home.md) · `this commit`
- **Script run form: argument descriptions shown again** — one description written as an f-string hid every description of the script (`dns_lookuptime`, `zap_digit`, `validation`, the game scripts) · [BUG-0278](../bugs/BUG-0278-2026-10-04-script-run-form-lost-every-argument-description.md) · `this commit`
- **10-minute archive chunks hold exactly their 10 minutes again, and no minute is transcribed twice** — each 1-minute piece is named after the minute it was recorded in, not the clock when the busy archiver got to it; on vpt-pi1 one merge in 5 to 23 % wrote a minute slot twice and skipped the next, and chunks came out 540 or 660 s. A host deploy must restart `vpt-archiver` and `vpt-transcript` · [BUG-0277](../bugs/BUG-0277-2026-10-04-archiver-wrote-a-minute-slot-twice-and-skipped-the-next.md) · `this commit`
- **Atlas analytics failed on every question asked through the Pi-hosted server** — servers without a database connection now forward the query to the main server · [BUG-0276](../bugs/BUG-0276-2026-10-04-atlas-analytics-failed-on-the-pi-hosted-server.md) · `47d971fc9`, `06aa2cbd5`, `510bd4281`
- **execute_testcase no longer reports a running testcase as failed** — a status read that times out is read again instead of ending the wait, so a caller does not start the next testcase on a busy device · [BUG-0275](../bugs/BUG-0275-2026-10-04-one-slow-status-read-ended-execute-testcase.md) · `this commit`
- **Text checks on a busy box take a fraction of a second again** — OCR runs single-threaded; on a loaded Raspberry Pi the same check took 0.2 to 30 s and could time out empty, now 0.2 s on an area and 0.7 s on the full frame · [BUG-0274](../bugs/BUG-0274-2026-10-04-ocr-took-up-to-30-s-on-a-loaded-host.md) · `this commit`
- **A slow OCR check no longer fails the image check next to it** — in a verification with several checks, each check's time window counts from when frames were captured, not from how long the other checks took to run; a 17 s OCR on a busy box had closed a 10 s image check before it saw the screen change · [BUG-0273](../bugs/BUG-0273-2026-10-04-a-slow-ocr-leg-closed-a-sibling-image-legs-window.md) · `this commit`
- **A passing image verification made the testcase status answer HTTP 500 and left the device locked** — numpy values are converted to plain JSON types for every verification and execution event; appear-then-disappear image checks can pass again · [BUG-0270](../bugs/BUG-0270-2026-10-03-numpy-value-500d-a-passing-testcase-and-kept-the-device-locked.md) · `9d3bd5b79`, `5f53a098a`
- **The BLE remote woke a stale bond, and keys that reached no box were reported as sent** — the wake aims at the bond the box last connected with and tries the others; a key with no connected box after the wake now fails · [BUG-0271](../bugs/BUG-0271-2026-10-03-ble-remote-woke-a-stale-bond-and-keys-reported-sent.md) · `d8d100372`, `5666223f7`
- **MCP create_node dropped verifications, and edge writes were never validated** — verifications are saved and validated, label is optional, the userinterface lookup uses the real route · [BUG-0272](../bugs/BUG-0272-2026-10-03-mcp-create-node-dropped-verifications-and-edge-validation-never-ran.md) · `e3d7e6b63`
- **AI Generate on Android finds the short way back** — validation stored the whole entry edge (close, launch, tap Home) as each tab's way home although the tap alone worked, and on a screen without the app's Home tab it tapped Android's Home button, left the app and saved no way back; it now keeps only the taps it replayed, and only taps elements of the app's own window · [BUG-0269](../bugs/BUG-0269-2026-10-04-ai-generate-validation-wrote-an-app-restart-as-the-way-back.md) · `this commit`
- **Screenshots showed nginx 401** — capture images under a host's stream path now get their access cookie on their own, also after it expires, and a screenshot opened in a new tab gets it before it opens (vpt-pi1, vpt-pi3, vpt-nipogi) · [BUG-0268](../bugs/BUG-0268-2026-10-04-screenshots-401-on-gated-host-paths.md) · `16f33780a`, `0436f415c`, `af1f318e4`
- **Mobile app showed only one server** — the app re-reads the server list on every launch instead of only at setup (APK 1.0.48) · [BUG-0267](../bugs/BUG-0267-2026-10-03-mobile-app-server-picker-shows-only-one-server.md)
- **Atlas made up the rows of an export and struggled to compare variants** — exports now hand the model the real file; a comparison needs only the two variants; Grafana drafts only on request · [BUG-0266](../bugs/BUG-0266-2026-10-03-atlas-invented-exported-rows-and-could-not-compare-variants.md) · `24dae71f4`, `49e44bd38`
- **Atlas answered chart and statistics questions without its analytics tools** — such questions now always get them · [BUG-0265](../bugs/BUG-0265-2026-10-03-atlas-answered-analytics-questions-without-the-analytics-tools.md) · `26c9672f3`
- **Atlas live status questions always failed** — the live-status adapter crashed on every call · [BUG-0260](../bugs/BUG-0260-2026-10-03-atlas-live-status-questions-always-failed.md) · `8ee5bd2bc`
- **Atlas KPI charts filtered by device flag failed** — the flag filter queried a column that does not exist · [BUG-0261](../bugs/BUG-0261-2026-10-03-atlas-kpi-flag-filter-referenced-a-missing-column.md) · `8ee5bd2bc`
- **Atlas network charts had no latency and mixed test types** — now one source per test with the dashboard's fields · [BUG-0262](../bugs/BUG-0262-2026-10-03-atlas-network-latency-was-always-empty.md) · `8ee5bd2bc`
- **Atlas "degraded hosts" listed devices that had since recovered** — it now reads the newest fleet check, like the dashboard · [BUG-0263](../bugs/BUG-0263-2026-10-03-atlas-degraded-hosts-counted-history.md) · `8ee5bd2bc`
- **Atlas KPI radar and occupancy differed from Grafana** — same grouping, environment, range clipping and PASS/FAIL defaults as the dashboards, checked panel by panel · [BUG-0264](../bugs/BUG-0264-2026-10-03-atlas-kpi-and-occupancy-numbers-differed-from-the-dashboards.md) · `c31a83788`
- **Jira instance settings ignored the plugins.jira permissions** — listing now needs `plugins.jira:view`, adding / editing / deleting needs `plugins.jira:manage`, like TestRail · [BUG-0238](../bugs/BUG-0238-2026-10-03-jira-instance-settings-were-open-to-every-signed-in-user.md)
- **A test case's execution history was always empty** — the query asked for a column `script_results` does not have and hid the error · [BUG-0237](../bugs/BUG-0237-2026-10-03-testcase-execution-history-was-always-empty.md) · `961a0769d`
- **AI Generate on Android proposes the app's own navigation** — it proposed the status bar, Android's navigation bar and the feed's tiles (`Four bars of phone signal`, `Recents`, a movie title) as screens, because the UI dump holds every window on screen; it now reads only the app's window, only what can be tapped, and prefers the tab bar and toolbar over list items (Google TV: Search, Shop, Library, Watchlist) · [BUG-0235](../bugs/BUG-0235-2026-10-02-ai-generate-on-android-proposed-the-status-bar-as-screens.md) · `this commit`
- **A node or edge saved through the API reaches the hosts** — create/update through `/server/navigationTrees/<tree>/nodes|edges` left every host on its cached graph until a manual cache clear (only the editor followed up); the routes now clear the hosts' graph like every other tree write · [BUG-0236](../bugs/BUG-0236-2026-10-02-edge-saved-through-the-api-left-hosts-on-the-old-graph.md) · `this commit`
- **Analyzer worker logs appear as they happen** — `vpt-discard-scripts` (Sherlock) buffered its output, so its lines reached the journal only when it restarted; the unit now sets `PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1`. The unit is hand-installed: add the line to `/etc/systemd/system/vpt-discard-scripts.service` and restart it · [BUG-0234](../bugs/BUG-0234-2026-10-02-analyzer-worker-logs-reached-the-journal-only-at-restart.md) · `790e21623`
- **Test Prompt: an exploration stops at its time limit** — the agent ran until it gave up on its own (15+ minutes on a hard prompt), keeping the device busy and overlapping the next run; it now stops at the skill's limit (5 min) and the run ends as an error saying so · [BUG-0233](../bugs/BUG-0233-2026-10-02-test-prompt-exploration-had-no-time-limit.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Builder: one Undo undoes one change** — the builder recorded a step for every redraw of the canvas (block sizes, selection, each mouse move of a drag), so one change such as an AI edit's Apply needed several Undo presses, and Undo itself added a step that broke Redo; it now records a step only when the blocks or connections change · [BUG-0232](../bugs/BUG-0232-2026-10-02-test-builder-undo-needed-several-presses.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Builder: a testcase keeps its interface when you pick the device** — opening a testcase from a link (e.g. Test Prompt's *Test Builder* button) and then picking a device switched the Interface to that device's first one (`web_tv` instead of `sauce-demo`) · [BUG-0231](../bugs/BUG-0231-2026-10-02-test-builder-loaded-testcase-got-the-wrong-interface.md) · `this commit`
- **The 24 h archive keeps every minute** — the host's stream writer deleted the oldest video segments while the archiver was merging them into each minute of the 10-minute archive, so whole minutes went missing (often a third of a chunk or more) and a click on the A/V quality timeline played black or showed a later moment. The writer now keeps about two minutes of segments, and a merge that loses a file keeps the rest. In plain words: replaying the last 24 h shows the moment you click, on every device; needs a host deploy · [BUG-0230](../bugs/BUG-0230-2026-10-02-archive-chunks-lose-whole-minutes.md)
- **A server deploy now restarts the heatmap and discard workers too** — `update_core.sh --server` restarted only `vpt-server`, so `vpt-heatmap` and the discard workers kept running the code they were started with; on the second server the heatmap ran a three-week-old processor that never wrote its status file, so `/status` and the System Service Status dashboard showed it as failing while it produced frames every minute. They are now restarted after `vpt-server`, only if already running. In plain words: deploying the server updates everything that runs on it, and a service you switched off stays off · [BUG-0229](../bugs/BUG-0229-2026-10-01-server-deploy-left-heatmap-and-discard-on-old-code.md)
- **Script report: the execution video fills the player** — a small recording (a web host's capture) played at its native size in the middle of an empty modal; the video now scales to the modal in both orientations · [BUG-0228](../bugs/BUG-0228-2026-10-01-report-video-modal-small-capture-not-scaled.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: Explore keeps the device under your control** — the exploring agent was told to force-unlock the device, so it cleared your Take Control on its first action; the next run was refused (*take_control_required*) while the page still showed you in control · [BUG-0227](../bugs/BUG-0227-2026-10-01-test-prompt-explore-force-unlocked-the-users-take-control.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: Explore reaches the target screen you picked** — the Target Screen dropdown saved the node's database id, which navigation does not know, so Explore failed at pre-navigation (*Target node … not found in unified graph*); it now saves the node id navigation uses. A prompt saved before this shows an empty Target until it is picked again · [BUG-0226](../bugs/BUG-0226-2026-10-01-test-prompt-target-saved-as-uuid-not-found-by-navigation.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: a saved prompt keeps its target screen** — opening a prompt wiped its Target Screen, so Explore from the page went to the tree's root and failed (*No unified path found to 'Entry'*), and saving stored the prompt without a target · [BUG-0225](../bugs/BUG-0225-2026-10-01-test-prompt-loaded-prompt-lost-its-target-screen.md) · `this commit`
- **Test Prompt: live steps show on the stream whatever the viewer's clock says** — the page compared the host's run start time with the browser's clock, so a browser more than 5 s ahead of the host never saw the step overlay; it now compares host times only · [BUG-0224](../bugs/BUG-0224-2026-10-01-test-prompt-overlay-hidden-by-clock-skew.md) · `this commit`
- **A script that crashes at startup now has a report and logs** — a syntax error, bad import or stray character at the top of a script used to leave the run empty; the host now records it as "script crashed before it started" with the Python error, and uploads the full traceback as its log · [BUG-0223](../bugs/BUG-0223-2026-10-01-script-crashing-at-startup-left-no-report-or-logs.md) · `this commit`
- **Standby measurement now times the wake after a long dwell** — after 5 min or more in standby the BLE cold-wake wakes the STB on its own, so the timed POWER press never happened and no KPI was recorded. The cold-wake is now the measured wake: from the BLE wake signal until the live screen (e.g. Home) appears, on the same wake edge and report · [BUG-0222](../bugs/BUG-0222-2026-10-01-standby-cold-wake-was-never-measured.md) · `this commit`
- **A KPI failure after "scan budget exceeded" shows its real scan window and frames** — the report said Scan Window 0.00s with no scan thumbnails or mosaic, because the frame list was dropped when the budget ran out; the error now also says the budget is processing time and how many frames were verified · [BUG-0221](../bugs/BUG-0221-2026-10-01-kpi-failure-report-scan-window-zero-on-budget-exceeded.md) · `this commit`
- **A device missed by the capture monitor at startup is now picked up within 10 s** — on a cascaded restart `vpt-monitor` could read the list of capture folders while `vpt-stream` was still writing it, and never read it again, so that device's frames were never analysed: no freeze / blackscreen / audio incident could fire, and vpt-pi4's desktop went 37 h unwatched while ffmpeg, the stall watcher and systemd all looked healthy. The monitor now re-reads the list every 10 s and starts watching any folder it was missing · [BUG-0220](../bugs/BUG-0220-2026-10-01-capture-monitor-missed-a-device-listed-after-it-started.md) · `this commit`
- **Deploys that update hosts run again** — after the lease check landed, a full, `--host` or `--restart` `update_core.sh` run stopped at once on *trim_host: command not found* and deployed nothing; `--frontend` and `--server` alone were not affected · [BUG-0219](../bugs/BUG-0219-2026-10-01-update-core-host-deploy-failed-on-trim-host.md) · `this commit`
- **2048 on a phone confirms Game over and finds the game screen reliably** — its checks ran OCR on the whole HDMI capture, which skipped the "Game over!" overlay and sometimes read nothing at all; they now read the board and the title row only · [BUG-0217](../bugs/BUG-0217-2026-10-01-game2048-phone-game-over-overlay-not-read-by-ocr.md)
- **2048 on a phone starts a new run right after a finished game** — New Game's "OK" step fails when there is no confirm dialog (after game over), and the run gave up although the new game was on screen; it now reads the screen and plays · [BUG-0218](../bugs/BUG-0218-2026-10-01-game2048-phone-run-after-a-finished-game-never-started.md)
- **Test Prompt: a saved prompt opens on its own interface** — clicking a prompt picked its device but showed that device's first interface (e.g. `web_tv` for a `sauce-demo` prompt), and the locked Interface picker could not be changed, so Take Control loaded the wrong tree · [BUG-0216](../bugs/BUG-0216-2026-10-01-test-prompt-loaded-prompt-got-the-wrong-interface.md) · `this commit`
- **KPI Measurement: the Action Set filter lists its action sets again** — since BUG-0157 its query failed with a GROUP BY error, so the drop-down was empty. In plain words: you can filter the KPI dashboard by action set again. Grafana dashboard: re-import `kpi-measurement.json` (no deploy script does it). [BUG-0214](../bugs/BUG-0214-2026-10-01-kpi-measurement-action-set-filter-query-error.md)
- **2048 on a phone no longer misreads the board and stops mid-game** — on the S21's HDMI capture the board finder sometimes took a square piece of the grid for the whole board, so a blind read returned tiles that weren't there and the run ended with `input_not_applied`; it now keeps the biggest square match · [BUG-0213](../bugs/BUG-0213-2026-10-01-game2048-phone-reader-took-a-piece-of-the-grid-for-the-board.md)
- **Test Prompt: "Save as testcase" links the testcase to the prompt** — the testcase was created but the link failed, so *Run graph* kept generating a new graph (an AI call) instead of replaying it; the host's testcase save and `execute-from-prompt` (with save) also returned an object where the testcase id belongs · [BUG-0215](../bugs/BUG-0215-2026-10-01-save-as-testcase-never-linked-the-prompt.md) · `5c32ad836`
- **Grafana's built-in admin can pick a workspace on dashboards** — the Workspace drop-down found the viewer by email only, and Grafana's built-in `admin` (email `admin@localhost`) has no VirtualPyTest account, so it offered only *None*. It now also reads the Grafana login and lists every workspace for `admin`, the same rule the device filter already used. In plain words: the admin can narrow dashboards to a workspace again. [BUG-0212](../bugs/BUG-0212-2026-10-01-grafana-workspace-dropdown-empty-for-builtin-admin.md) · 🗄 DB migration `20261001d_grafana_workspaces_builtin_admin.sql`
- **Stream-modal AI panel saves your choice for an ambiguous screen name** — choosing a screen with "remember" posted a route that does not exist, so the panel showed *Failed to apply disambiguation* and asked again next time; it now saves through the same route as the Test Builder · [BUG-0211](../bugs/BUG-0211-2026-10-01-stream-modal-ai-panel-could-not-save-disambiguation.md) · `this commit`
- **Tests generated from a prompt run their actions and checks** — a graph built by the AI (Test Prompt *Run graph*, the stream-modal AI panel, the Test Builder's AI generation) stopped at its first action or verification with *re-create the block*, because the AI builder still wrote blocks in an old shape and never gave a command its parameters (`press_key` with no key). It now writes the blocks the executor runs, with parameters (`press_key(key=OK)`, `waitForTextToAppear(text="Live TV")`), and graphs cached in the old shape are regenerated · [BUG-0210](../bugs/BUG-0210-2026-10-01-ai-generated-graphs-failed-on-every-action-and-verification.md) · `this commit`
- **AI Dataset: freeze strips show their frame times again** — the review page read each strip frame as `{t_rel_s}`, but the host stores plain seconds, so no strip showed its `+52.8 s · after freeze` chips and the reviewer had nothing to tell frozen frames from live ones · [BUG-0209](../bugs/BUG-0209-2026-10-01-freeze-strip-frame-chips-never-shown.md) · `this commit`
- **Running a testcase from a prompt works again** — `/server/testcase/execute-from-prompt` failed on every device with *does not have AIExecutor initialized*, because the host still called an AI executor that had been replaced by the graph builder. It now generates the graph with the builder, optionally saves it, and runs it like any other testcase (sync, or async with live execution events), holding the device for the whole run. `/server/testcase/generate-with-ai` now only generates, as documented, instead of going through that broken route. Neither route is called by a page today; the stream-modal AI panel uses `generatePlan` + `/testcase/execute` and was not affected ([BUG-0208](../bugs/BUG-0208-2026-10-01-execute-from-prompt-always-failed-on-missing-ai-executor.md))
- **One Run click no longer leaves a second "running forever" row** — a second click while the first was still launching queued a copy that waited behind the device lock and never finished; Run is now one launch per click, and a device under your own Take Control runs the script directly instead of queueing it · [BUG-0207](../bugs/BUG-0207-2026-10-01-one-run-click-left-a-phantom-queued-row.md) · `this commit`
- **Standby measurement reports carry their summary again** — the run measured the wake, then crashed building the summary on a helper whose signature had changed in July, so the report said "Execution summary not available". The host log also now says when an early device release was declined because a user holds Take Control · [BUG-0284](../bugs/BUG-0284-2026-10-01-standby-summary-crashed-on-kpi-helper-signature.md) · `this commit`
- **An Apple TV on an IRTrans box had no remote, and a Fire TV always showed an IR panel** — the remotes a device could get were hard-coded per model, so an Apple TV with `DEVICE{n}_IR_IP` set came up as *"Unsupported device: apple_tv"* and an Android TV could not use IR at all; Fire TV drew an IR panel even with no transmitter configured. Every HDMI/camera model now takes whichever IR, IRTrans or BLE remote its `.env` sets, `DEVICE{n}_IR_REMOTE` is optional, and the panels show only the remotes the host created · [BUG-0205](../bugs/BUG-0205-2026-10-01-ir-remote-refused-by-device-model.md) · `d47966813` `0c54bff88`
- **Fire TV IR sent no keys with `IR_TYPE=fire_tv`** — the key-code file was named `ir_conf/firet_v.json`, so the type that showed the Fire TV panel loaded no codes, and the one that loaded codes showed the Samsung panel. Renamed to `fire_tv.json`; **a host still set to `IR_TYPE=firet_v` must change it to `fire_tv`** · [BUG-0206](../bugs/BUG-0206-2026-10-01-fire-tv-ir-profile-misnamed.md)
- **Standby measurement no longer blames a missing KPI reference when the STB woke during the cold-wake** — after a long dwell the WoBLE cold-wake can wake the box all the way to live, so the measured wake edge never runs; the run now says so instead of sending you to edit KPI settings that were fine. KPI rows are also matched by the variant's action set id, not base's · [BUG-0204](../bugs/BUG-0204-2026-10-01-standby-wake-already-awake-blamed-kpi.md) · `this commit`
- **Runs under a variant were querying the database on every internal graph read** — resolving a variant name to its id happened per read, roughly 15 times per navigation step, and each of those queries also dragged every variant's full override JSON that the resolver never looks at. It is now fetched once per interface and held for a few seconds, dropped whenever the tree cache is invalidated so a rename still takes effect. Measured on the lab: **49 ms of database per navigation step down to 2 ms**, and it scales with override size and database latency · [BUG-0203](../bugs/BUG-0203-2026-09-30-variant-map-refetched-per-graph-read.md) · `67f83aad4`
- **Taking control now pre-builds the navigation graph for the variant you are looking at** — it always warmed the base scope, so the first run under a variant rebuilt the whole tree hierarchy mid-run even though the warm-up had just finished. Measured at 68 ms on a 14-tree lab interface, and it grows with hierarchy size and database latency; `scripts/measure_cache_warmup.py` reports it for any host · [BUG-0202](../bugs/BUG-0202-2026-09-30-takecontrol-warmed-the-wrong-cache-scope.md) · `0e0ef4882`
- **The event a run's result travels on is now delivered where it can be heard to fail** — a finished run handed its completion event to a detached thread nobody was left to check, so a rejected delivery produced no trace inside that run. Two silent outages in ten days turned on exactly that. Terminal events are now delivered on the thread that produced the result (which is exiting anyway, so nothing waits longer), progress events still never block the request handler, and a regression test pins the whole contract — credential sent, refusal logged, terminal blocking, progress not · [BUG-0201](../bugs/BUG-0201-2026-09-30-terminal-execution-events-were-fire-and-forget.md) · `9789e90cc`
- **A node's Run recorded its result against the wrong variant** — edge runs have always declared the scope they ran under; node verification never did, so the host used whatever variant an earlier unrelated request had left on the device. Switching the canvas variant and running a node check with no navigation in between filed the metric under the previous scope, and the Localize cross-check rebuilt the whole tree hierarchy every time because it was looking for a scope the cache had not been asked to hold. The scope is now declared with the run, so the metric lands where it belongs and the lookup is a cache hit · [BUG-0200](../bugs/BUG-0200-2026-09-30-node-verification-runs-under-a-leftover-variant.md) · `6ab94a9a0`
- **Script progress, early device release and deployment status were being dropped without a word** — three host→server callbacks either sent no credential or ignored the server's answer, and because a rejection is a response rather than an error they read as delivered. On a server that requires a credential that meant live progress never updated during a run, a device stayed locked through the report/video/upload tail instead of being released early, and a refused deployment notification looked identical to a delivered one. All three now authenticate and say so when they are refused. Same defect as [BUG-0151], whose sweep stopped at four callers · [BUG-0199](../bugs/BUG-0199-2026-09-30-three-more-host-callbacks-swallow-401.md) · `166fc0a5f`
- **A verification that times out or cannot run now says so in the Edit Node panel** — when a run produced no results the panel simply stayed empty and the only explanation was a toast that faded, so it looked as though nothing had happened. The reason now renders where the results would have been, in the same red box, and reads the same in both places — *"Verification timed out — the host did not report a result in time"* rather than a raw error string · `a9b1db893`
- **A host's data disk filled up, the dashboard read 9.8%, and verifications blamed the device for it** — the host only measured `/`, which on a host that keeps captures, references and reports on their own mount is the one filesystem that cannot fill: 960 GB at 100% while the tile stayed green.
